## Step 1.2: Task Understanding

**My understanding:** The vault MCP server (cloud-vault-mcp) exposes tools like `vault_read`, `vault_write`, `vault_search`, etc. When Claude Code calls these tools, they fail with a "Session not found" error. This likely means the MCP server has a session management layer that requires sessions to be created/tracked before tool calls work, and either sessions aren't being created, are expiring, or the session lookup logic is broken.

Let me investigate the vault MCP server code to find the root cause.

   503→                row_num: Row number (1-based, row 2 = first data row)
   504→                status: Research status (e.g. 'Researched', 'Inaccessible')
   505→                abstractions: Key abstractions (1-2 sentences)
   506→                domain: Domain category (e.g. 'AI Architecture', 'Astrophysics')
   507→                integration_point: Relevant Cohezion module
   508→            """
   509→            try:
   510→                result = sheets.update_row(
   511→                    row_num, status, abstractions, domain, integration_point
   512→                )
   513→                return json.dumps(result, indent=2)
   514→            except Exception as e:
   515→                return f"Error: {e}"
   516→
   517→        @mcp.tool()
   518→        def sheets_batch_update(data: list[dict]) -> str:
   519→            """Batch update multiple ranges in Cohezion_Research.
   520→
   521→            Args:
   522→                data: List of {range: 'Sheet1!B2:E2', values: [['v1', ...]]}
   523→            """
   524→            try:
   525→                result = sheets.batch_update(data)
   526→                return json.dumps(result, indent=2)
   527→            except Exception as e:
   528→                return f"Error: {e}"
   529→
   530→        @mcp.tool()
   531→        def sheets_update_vault_note(row_num: int, vault_note: str) -> str:
   532→            """Update column F (Vault Note) for a row.
   533→
   534→            Args:
   535→                row_num: Row number (1-based)
   536→                vault_note: Vault note filename (e.g. 'agentic-ai-memory-hierarchies.md')
   537→            """
   538→            try:
   539→                result = sheets.update_vault_note_column(row_num, vault_note)
   540→                return json.dumps(result, indent=2)
   541→            except Exception as e:
   542→                return f"Error: {e}"
   543→
   544→    # ── SurrealDB Graph Database Sync ────────────────────────────────
   545→
   546→    if surrealdb:
   547→
   548→        @mcp.tool()
   549→        def surrealdb_import_papers() -> str:
   550→            """Import all papers from vault/papers/ to SurrealDB.
   551→
   552→            Performs bulk import of all paper markdown files, extracting:
   553→            - Frontmatter metadata (title, date, tags)
   554→            - Wiki-links to concepts
   555→            - Content for indexing
   556→
   557→            Returns count of papers imported.
   558→            """
   559→            try:
   560→                count = surrealdb.bulk_import_papers()
   561→                return f"Successfully imported {count} papers to SurrealDB"
   562→            except Exception as e:
   563→                logger.error(f"Failed to import papers: {e}")
   564→                return f"Error importing papers: {e}"
   565→
   566→        @mcp.tool()
   567→        def surrealdb_import_concepts() -> str:
   568→            """Import all concepts from vault/concepts/ to SurrealDB.
   569→
   570→            Performs bulk import of all concept markdown files, extracting:
   571→            - Frontmatter metadata (title, tags)
   572→            - Content for indexing
   573→
   574→            Returns count of concepts imported.
   575→            """
   576→            try:
   577→                count = surrealdb.bulk_import_concepts()
   578→                return f"Successfully imported {count} concepts to SurrealDB"
   579→            except Exception as e:
   580→                logger.error(f"Failed to import concepts: {e}")
   581→                return f"Error importing concepts: {e}"
   582→
   583→        @mcp.tool()
   584→        def surrealdb_start_watching() -> str:
   585→            """Start real-time file watching for vault changes.
   586→
   587→            Monitors papers/, concepts/, patterns/, and decisions/ directories
   588→            for file modifications and creations. Changes are automatically
   589→            synced to SurrealDB in real-time.
   590→
   591→            This enables live updates to the 12D graph visualization.
   592→            """
   593→            try:
   594→                surrealdb.start_watching()
   595→                return "File watcher started - vault changes will sync to SurrealDB"
   596→            except Exception as e:
   597→                logger.error(f"Failed to start file watcher: {e}")
   598→                return f"Error starting file watcher: {e}"
   599→
   600→        @mcp.tool()
   601→        def surrealdb_stop_watching() -> str:
   602→            """Stop real-time file watching."""
   603→            try:
   604→                surrealdb.stop_watching()
   605→                return "File watcher stopped"
   606→            except Exception as e:
   607→                logger.error(f"Failed to stop file watcher: {e}")
   608→                return f"Error stopping file watcher: {e}"
   609→
   610→        @mcp.tool()
   611→        def surrealdb_query(query: str) -> str:
   612→            """Execute a custom SurrealQL query against the vault graph database.
   613→
   614→            Args:
   615→                query: SurrealQL query string (automatically prefixed with USE NS/DB)
   616→
   617→            Returns:
   618→                Query results as JSON
   619→
   620→            Example queries:
   621→            - "SELECT * FROM paper WHERE tags CONTAINS 'ai' LIMIT 10;"
   622→            - "SELECT count() FROM links GROUP BY out;"
   623→            - "SELECT * FROM paper->links->concept WHERE out.title = 'agentic-ai';"
   624→            """
   625→            try:
   626→                # Prepend USE statements
   627→                full_query = f"USE NS {surrealdb.namespace}; USE DB {surrealdb.database}; {query}"
   628→                results = surrealdb._execute_query(full_query)
   629→                return json.dumps(results, indent=2, default=str)
   630→            except Exception as e:
   631→                logger.error(f"Query failed: {e}")
   632→                return f"Error executing query: {e}"
   633→
   634→    # ── Ollama Integration ────────────────────────────────────────────
   635→
   636→    if config.ollama_enabled:
   637→        # Import Ollama client for direct integration
   638→        try:
   639→            from .ollama_client import OllamaClient
   640→
   641→            ollama_client = OllamaClient(
   642→                base_url=config.ollama_url,
   643→                timeout=config.ollama_timeout,
   644→            )
   645→
   646→            @mcp.tool()
   647→            async def ollama_query(
   648→                prompt: str,
   649→                model: str = "auto",
   650→                temperature: float = 0.7,
   651→            ) -> str:
   652→                """Execute a query against Ollama model.
   653→
   654→                Args:
   655→                    prompt: The prompt/question to send to the model
   656→                    model: Model to use (use "auto" for automatic selection)
   657→                    temperature: Temperature for generation (0.0-1.0)
   658→
   659→                Returns:
   660→                    Generated text response from the model
   661→                """
   662→                try:
   663→                    response = await ollama_client.query(prompt, model, temperature)
   664→                    return response or "Empty response from Ollama"
   665→                except Exception as e:
   666→                    logger.error(f"Ollama query failed: {e}")
   667→                    return f"Error: {e}"
   668→
   669→            @mcp.tool()
   670→            async def ollama_embed(
   671→                texts: list[str],
   672→                model: str = "nomic-embed-text:latest",
   673→            ) -> str:
   674→                """Generate embeddings for texts.
   675→
   676→                Args:
   677→                    texts: List of text strings to embed
   678→                    model: Embedding model to use
   679→
   680→                Returns:
   681→                    List of embedding vectors as JSON
   682→                """
   683→                try:
   684→                    embeddings = await ollama_client.embed(texts, model)
   685→                    return json.dumps(embeddings)
   686→                except Exception as e:
   687→                    logger.error(f"Ollama embed failed: {e}")
   688→                    return f"Error: {e}"
   689→
   690→            @mcp.tool()
   691→            async def ollama_status() -> str:
   692→                """Get Ollama service status and available models.
   693→
   694→                Returns:
   695→                    Status information including loaded models
   696→                """
   697→                try:
   698→                    status = await ollama_client.status()
   699→                    return json.dumps(status)
   700→                except Exception as e:
   701→                    logger.error(f"Ollama status check failed: {e}")
   702→                    return f"Error: {e}"
   703→
   704→        except ImportError:
   705→            logger.warning("Ollama client not available for direct integration")
   706→
   707→    # ── Health Check Operations ────────────────────────────────────────
   708→
   709→    if health_checker:
   710→
   711→        @mcp.tool()
   712→        async def vault_health_check() -> str:
   713→            """Check health of all MCP dependencies.
   714→
   715→            Tests the following services:
   716→            - Vault filesystem (read/write access)
   717→            - SurrealDB graph database
   718→            - Google Sheets API
   719→            - Ollama service
   720→            - Disk space
   721→            - Process memory usage
   722→
   723→            Returns a detailed status report with latencies and connection status.
   724→            """
   725→            try:
   726→                status = await health_checker.run_all_checks(timeout=5)
   727→                return json.dumps(status.to_dict(), indent=2)
   728→            except Exception as e:
   729→                logger.error(f"Health check failed: {e}")
   730→                return json.dumps(
   731→                    {
   732→                        "status": "unhealthy",
   733→                        "error": str(e),
   734→                        "timestamp": None,
   735→                    },
   736→                    indent=2,
   737→                )
   738→
   739→    # ── Agent Context Operations (SurrealDB Phase 1) ────────────────────
   740→
   741→    if agent_context:
   742→
   743→        @mcp.tool()
   744→        def track_session(
   745→            agent_id: str,
   746→            goals: list[str],
   747→            model_used: str = "claude-haiku-4-5",
   748→            phase: str = "research",
   749→        ) -> str:
   750→            """Track the start of an agent session for research lineage.
   751→
   752→            Creates an agent_session node in SurrealDB to capture session metadata,
   753→            goals, resource usage, and status.
   754→
   755→            Args:
   756→                agent_id: Unique agent identifier (e.g., 'integration-engineer')
   757→                goals: List of goals for this session
   758→                model_used: LLM model name (default: claude-haiku-4-5)
   759→                phase: Session phase (research, decision, implementation, validation)
   760→
   761→            Returns:
   762→                JSON with session_id, status, timestamp on success;
   763→                error message on failure
   764→            """
   765→            try:
   766→                result = agent_context.track_session(agent_id, goals, model_used, phase)
   767→                return json.dumps(result, indent=2)
   768→            except Exception as e:
   769→                logger.error(f"Error tracking session: {e}")
   770→                return json.dumps({"success": False, "error": str(e)}, indent=2)
   771→
   772→        @mcp.tool()
   773→        def record_decision(
   774→            session_id: str,
   775→            decision_type: str,
   776→            reasoning: str,
   777→            papers_applied: list[str],
   778→            confidence_score: float = 0.7,
   779→        ) -> str:
   780→            """Record an architectural decision with research lineage.
   781→
   782→            Creates an agent_decision node and APPLIED_RESEARCH edges linking
   783→            the decision to research papers that informed it.
   784→
   785→            Args:
   786→                session_id: Session ID from track_session
   787→                decision_type: Type (architecture, feature, refactor, bugfix, data)
   788→                reasoning: Natural language explanation of the decision
   789→                papers_applied: List of paper IDs that informed this decision
   790→                confidence_score: Confidence level (0-1)
   791→
   792→            Returns:
   793→                JSON with decision_id, links_created, timestamp on success;
   794→                validation_warnings and error messages on partial failures
   795→            """
   796→            try:
   797→                result = agent_context.record_decision(
   798→                    session_id, decision_type, reasoning, papers_applied, confidence_score
   799→                )
   800→                return json.dumps(result, indent=2)
   801→            except Exception as e:
   802→                logger.error(f"Error recording decision: {e}")
   803→                return json.dumps({"success": False, "error": str(e)}, indent=2)
   804→
   805→        @mcp.tool()
   806→        def record_outcome(
   807→            session_id: str,
   808→            outcome_type: str,
   809→            lessons_learned: list[str],
   810→            metrics: dict = None,
   811→        ) -> str:
   812→            """Record session outcome and validate against lessons learned.
   813→
   814→            Creates an agent_outcome node and VALIDATES_LESSON edges linking
   815→            the outcome to lessons from the vault. Also closes the session
   816→            and marks it as completed.
   817→
   818→            Args:
   819→                session_id: Session ID from track_session
   820→                outcome_type: Type (success, partial, failed)
   821→                lessons_learned: List of lesson note IDs from vault
   822→                metrics: Dict of outcome metrics (session_duration_min, token_efficiency_ratio, etc)
   823→
   824→            Returns:
   825→                JSON with outcome_id, validated_lessons, timestamp on success;
   826→                validation_errors for missing lessons on partial failures
   827→            """
   828→            try:
   829→                result = agent_context.record_outcome(
   830→                    session_id, outcome_type, lessons_learned, metrics
   831→                )
   832→                return json.dumps(result, indent=2)
   833→            except Exception as e:
   834→                logger.error(f"Error recording outcome: {e}")
   835→                return json.dumps({"success": False, "error": str(e)}, indent=2)
   836→
   837→    # ── Pocket TTS (Text-to-Speech) ─────────────────────────────────
   838→    try:

   843→        @mcp.tool()

   848→                text: Text to synthesize (max 4096 characters)

   855→            return json.dumps(result, indent=2)

   857→    except ImportError:

     2→
     3→import asyncio
     4→import logging
     5→from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
     6→
     7→import uvicorn
     8→from starlette.applications import Starlette
     9→from starlette.middleware.trustedhost import TrustedHostMiddleware
    10→from starlette.requests import Request

    14→from .config import ServerConfig
    15→from .health import HealthChecker
    16→from .server import create_server

    18→from .vault_watcher import VaultFileWatcher
    19→
    20→
    21→# Import security modules
    22→try:
    23→    from cohezion.security.https_middleware import create_https_app
    24→    from cohezion.security.tls_config import TLSConfig
    25→except ImportError:
    26→    TLSConfig = None
    27→    create_https_app = None
    28→
    29→
    30→logger = logging.getLogger("cloud-vault-mcp")
    31→
    32→
    33→def main():
    34→    """Run the MCP server."""
    35→    logging.basicConfig(
    36→        level=logging.INFO,
    37→        format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s",
    38→    )
    39→
    40→    config = ServerConfig.from_env()
    41→
    42→    if not config.api_key:
    43→        logger.warning(
    44→            "MCP_API_KEY is not set. The server will run without authentication. "
    45→            "Set MCP_API_KEY environment variable for production use."
    46→        )
    47→
    48→    logger.info("Vault path: %s", config.vault_path)
    49→    logger.info("Starting Cloud Vault MCP Server on %s:%d", config.host, config.port)
    50→
    51→    mcp = create_server(config)
    52→
    53→    # Initialize health checker
    54→    health_checker = None
    55→    if config.health_check_enabled:
    56→        health_checker = HealthChecker(
    57→            vault_path=config.vault_path,
    58→            surrealdb_url=config.surrealdb_url,
    59→            ollama_url=config.ollama_url,
    60→        )
    61→        logger.info("Health check enabled")
    62→
    63→    # FastMCP provides factory methods to build ASGI apps - call streamable_http_app()
    64→    mcp_app = mcp.streamable_http_app()
    65→
    66→    # Add TrustedHostMiddleware if not accepting all hosts
    67→    if "*" not in config.allowed_hosts:
    68→        mcp_app = TrustedHostMiddleware(mcp_app, allowed_hosts=config.allowed_hosts)
    69→
    70→    # Create health endpoint handler
    71→    async def health_endpoint(request: Request):
    72→        """Health check endpoint."""
    73→        if health_checker is None:
    74→            return JSONResponse(
    75→                {"error": "Health check not enabled"},
    76→                status_code=503,
    77→            )
    78→
    79→        try:
    80→            status = await health_checker.run_all_checks(
    81→                timeout=int(config.health_check_timeout)
    82→            )
    83→            status_code = 200 if status.status == "healthy" else 503
    84→            return JSONResponse(
    85→                content=status.to_dict(),
    86→                status_code=status_code,
    87→            )
    88→        except Exception as e:
    89→            logger.error(f"Health check failed: {e}")
    90→            return JSONResponse(
    91→                {
    92→                    "status": "unhealthy",
    93→                    "error": str(e),
    94→                    "timestamp": None,
    95→                },
    96→                status_code=503,
    97→            )
    98→
    99→    if config.watcher_enabled:
   100→        # Create watcher and SSE stream
   101→        loop = asyncio.new_event_loop()
   102→        watcher = VaultFileWatcher(config.vault_path, loop, debounce_seconds=0.5)
   103→        sse = VaultEventStream(watcher, heartbeat_seconds=config.sse_heartbeat_seconds)
   104→
   105→        @asynccontextmanager
   106→        async def lifespan(app):
   107→            # Reassign loop to the running event loop
   108→            nonlocal watcher
   109→            running_loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
   110→            watcher = VaultFileWatcher(
   111→                config.vault_path, running_loop, debounce_seconds=0.5
   112→            )
   113→            sse._watcher = watcher
   114→            watcher.start()
   115→            logger.info("VaultFileWatcher started")
   116→            yield
   117→            watcher.stop()
   118→            logger.info("VaultFileWatcher stopped")
   119→
   120→        # Create base Starlette app with SSE and health routes with lifespan
   121→        sse_app = Starlette(
   122→            routes=[
   123→                Route("/events/vault", sse.sse_endpoint),
   124→                Route("/health", health_endpoint),
   125→            ],
   126→            lifespan=lifespan,
   127→        )
   128→
   129→        # Wrap with MCP fallback for all other routes
   130→        async def app(scope, receive, send):
   131→            if scope["type"] == "http":
   132→                if scope["path"] == "/events/vault":
   133→                    # Route to SSE handler
   134→                    await sse_app(scope, receive, send)
   135→                elif scope["path"] == "/health":
   136→                    # Route to health check
   137→                    await sse_app(scope, receive, send)
   138→                else:
   139→                    # Route to MCP
   140→                    await mcp_app(scope, receive, send)
   141→            elif scope["type"] == "lifespan":
   142→                # Handle lifespan through sse_app
   143→                await sse_app(scope, receive, send)
   144→            else:
   145→                # WebSocket or other protocol
   146→                await mcp_app(scope, receive, send)
   147→    else:
   148→        # Create Starlette app with just health endpoint (no watcher)
   149→        health_app = Starlette(
   150→            routes=[Route("/health", health_endpoint)],
   151→        )
   152→
   153→        async def app(scope, receive, send):
   154→            if scope["type"] == "http":
   155→                if scope["path"] == "/health":
   156→                    # Route to health check
   157→                    await health_app(scope, receive, send)
   158→                else:
   159→                    # Route to MCP
   160→                    await mcp_app(scope, receive, send)
   161→            else:
   162→                # WebSocket or other protocol
   163→                await mcp_app(scope, receive, send)
   164→
   165→    # Apply HTTPS middleware if TLS is enabled
   166→    if config.tls_enabled and TLSConfig and create_https_app:
   167→        logger.info("Configuring HTTPS/TLS security")
   168→
   169→        tls_config = TLSConfig(
   170→            cert_path=config.tls_cert_path,
   171→            key_path=config.tls_key_path,
   172→            hsts_max_age=config.tls_hsts_max_age,
   173→            allowed_origins=config.tls_allowed_origins,
   174→        )
   175→
   176→        if not tls_config.validate_certificate():
   177→            logger.error(
   178→                "TLS certificate validation failed. "
   179→                "Proceeding with HTTP (NOT RECOMMENDED FOR PRODUCTION)"
   180→            )
   181→        else:
   182→            logger.info("TLS certificate validated successfully")
   183→            app = create_https_app(
   184→                app, tls_config, allow_http_localhost=True
   185→            )
   186→
   187→    # Run with uvicorn directly to control host/port
   188→    # Note: For HTTPS, use ssl_certfile and ssl_keyfile parameters
   189→    ssl_certfile = None
   190→    ssl_keyfile = None
   191→
   192→    if config.tls_enabled and config.tls_cert_path and config.tls_key_path:
   193→        ssl_certfile = config.tls_cert_path
   194→        ssl_keyfile = config.tls_key_path
   195→        logger.info("Starting HTTPS server with SSL certificates")
   196→
   197→    uvicorn.run(

   199→        host=config.host,
   200→        port=config.port,
   201→        log_level="info",
   202→        ssl_certfile=ssl_certfile,
   203→        ssl_keyfile=ssl_keyfile,
   204→        http="h11",
   205→    )

> AGENT

There it is — **"Session not found"**. The error occurs when the client sends a session ID that the server doesn't recognize. This happens when the server restarts (losing in-memory sessions) but Claude Code retains and resends the old session ID. Let me check the FastMCP source to understand the session lifecycle:

> TOOL

   202→        self,
   203→        scope: Scope,
   204→        receive: Receive,
   205→        send: Send,
   206→    ) -> None:
   207→        """
   208→        Process request in stateful mode - maintaining session state between requests.
   209→
   210→        Args:

   212→            receive: ASGI receive function
   213→            send: ASGI send function

   215→        request = Request(scope, receive)
   216→        request_mcp_session_id = request.headers.get(MCP_SESSION_ID_HEADER)
   217→
   218→        # Existing session case
   219→        if request_mcp_session_id is not None and request_mcp_session_id in self._server_instances:  # pragma: no cover

   224→
   225→        if request_mcp_session_id is None:
   226→            # New session case

   228→            async with self._session_creation_lock:

   231→                    mcp_session_id=new_session_id,

     1→"""StreamableHTTP Session Manager for MCP servers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import contextlib
     6→import logging
     7→from collections.abc import AsyncIterator
     8→from http import HTTPStatus
     9→from typing import Any
    10→from uuid import uuid4
    11→
    12→import anyio
    13→from anyio.abc import TaskStatus
    14→from starlette.requests import Request
    15→from starlette.responses import Response
    16→from starlette.types import Receive, Scope, Send
    17→
    18→from mcp.server.lowlevel.server import Server as MCPServer
    19→from mcp.server.streamable_http import (
    20→    MCP_SESSION_ID_HEADER,
    21→    EventStore,
    22→    StreamableHTTPServerTransport,
    23→)
    24→from mcp.server.transport_security import TransportSecuritySettings
    25→from mcp.types import INVALID_REQUEST, ErrorData, JSONRPCError
    26→
    27→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    28→
    29→
    30→class StreamableHTTPSessionManager:
    31→    """
    32→    Manages StreamableHTTP sessions with optional resumability via event store.
    33→
    34→    This class abstracts away the complexity of session management, event storage,
    35→    and request handling for StreamableHTTP transports. It handles:
    36→
    37→    1. Session tracking for clients
    38→    2. Resumability via an optional event store
    39→    3. Connection management and lifecycle
    40→    4. Request handling and transport setup
    41→
    42→    Important: Only one StreamableHTTPSessionManager instance should be created
    43→    per application. The instance cannot be reused after its run() context has
    44→    completed. If you need to restart the manager, create a new instance.
    45→
    46→    Args:
    47→        app: The MCP server instance
    48→        event_store: Optional event store for resumability support.
    49→                     If provided, enables resumable connections where clients
    50→                     can reconnect and receive missed events.
    51→                     If None, sessions are still tracked but not resumable.
    52→        json_response: Whether to use JSON responses instead of SSE streams
    53→        stateless: If True, creates a completely fresh transport for each request
    54→                   with no session tracking or state persistence between requests.
    55→        security_settings: Optional transport security settings.
    56→        retry_interval: Retry interval in milliseconds to suggest to clients in SSE
    57→                       retry field. Used for SSE polling behavior.
    58→    """
    59→
    60→    def __init__(
    61→        self,
    62→        app: MCPServer[Any, Any],
    63→        event_store: EventStore | None = None,
    64→        json_response: bool = False,
    65→        stateless: bool = False,
    66→        security_settings: TransportSecuritySettings | None = None,
    67→        retry_interval: int | None = None,
    68→    ):
    69→        self.app = app
    70→        self.event_store = event_store
    71→        self.json_response = json_response
    72→        self.stateless = stateless
    73→        self.security_settings = security_settings
    74→        self.retry_interval = retry_interval
    75→
    76→        # Session tracking (only used if not stateless)
    77→        self._session_creation_lock = anyio.Lock()
    78→        self._server_instances: dict[str, StreamableHTTPServerTransport] = {}
    79→
    80→        # The task group will be set during lifespan

   130→        receive: Receive,

   132→    ) -> None:
   133→        """
   134→        Process ASGI request with proper session handling and transport setup.
   135→
   136→        Dispatches to the appropriate handler based on stateless mode.
   137→
   138→        Args:
   139→            scope: ASGI scope
   140→            receive: ASGI receive function
   141→            send: ASGI send function
   142→        """
   143→        if self._task_group is None:
   144→            raise RuntimeError("Task group is not initialized. Make sure to use run().")
   145→
   146→        # Dispatch to the appropriate handler
   147→        if self.stateless:
   148→            await self._handle_stateless_request(scope, receive, send)
   149→        else:
   150→            await self._handle_stateful_request(scope, receive, send)
   151→
   152→    async def _handle_stateless_request(
   153→        self,
   154→        scope: Scope,
   155→        receive: Receive,
   156→        send: Send,
   157→    ) -> None:
   158→        """
   159→        Process request in stateless mode - creating a new transport for each request.
   160→
   161→        Args:
   162→            scope: ASGI scope
   163→            receive: ASGI receive function
   164→            send: ASGI send function
   165→        """
   166→        logger.debug("Stateless mode: Creating new transport for this request")
   167→        # No session ID needed in stateless mode
   168→        http_transport = StreamableHTTPServerTransport(
   169→            mcp_session_id=None,  # No session tracking in stateless mode
   170→            is_json_response_enabled=self.json_response,
   171→            event_store=None,  # No event store in stateless mode
   172→            security_settings=self.security_settings,
   173→        )
   174→
   175→        # Start server in a new task
   176→        async def run_stateless_server(*, task_status: TaskStatus[None] = anyio.TASK_STATUS_IGNORED):
   177→            async with http_transport.connect() as streams:
   178→                read_stream, write_stream = streams
   179→                task_status.started()
   180→                try:
   181→                    await self.app.run(
   182→                        read_stream,
   183→                        write_stream,
   184→                        self.app.create_initialization_options(),
   185→                        stateless=True,
   186→                    )
   187→                except Exception:  # pragma: no cover
   188→                    logger.exception("Stateless session crashed")

When the server restarts (or the systemd service crashes and a manual `run_mcp.py` takes over), Claude Code retains the old session ID and sends it — but the new server instance doesn't recognize it, returning HTTP 404 \"Session not found\". The fix is to enable `stateless_http=True` on the FastMCP server, since all vault tools are stateless request/response operations that don't need server-side session persistence.\n\n**Tech Stack:** Python, FastMCP (mcp==1.26.0), Starlette/ASGI\n\n## Root Cause Analysis\n\n1. **`server.py:61`** creates `FastMCP(\"Cloud Vault\", ...)` without `stateless_http=True`\n2. In stateful mode, `_handle_stateful_request()` requires clients to either:\n   - Send NO session ID (creates a new session)\n   - Send a VALID session ID (routes to existing session)\n4.   "new_string": "**Architecture:** The MCP Python SDK's `StreamableHTTPSessionManager` runs in stateful mode by default, assigning each client a session ID stored in server memory. When the server restarts (or the systemd service crashes and a manual `run_mcp.py` takes over), Claude Code retains the old session ID and sends it — but the new server instance doesn't recognize it, returning HTTP 404 \"Session not found\". The fix is to enable `stateless_http=True` on the FastMCP server. In stateless mode, `_handle_stateless_request()` ignores incoming `Mcp-Session-Id` headers entirely and creates a fresh transport per request — verified in `mcp/server/streamable_http_manager.py:152-196`."
}

     1→# Fix Vault MCP "Session not found" Error
     2→
     3→Created: 2026-02-23
     4→Status: PENDING
     5→Approved: Yes
     6→Iterations: 0
     7→Worktree: Yes
     8→
     9→> **Status Lifecycle:** PENDING → COMPLETE → VERIFIED
    10→> **Iterations:** Tracks implement→verify cycles (incremented by verify phase)
    11→>
    12→> - PENDING: Initial state, awaiting implementation
    13→> - COMPLETE: All tasks implemented
    14→> - VERIFIED: All checks passed
    15→>
    16→> **Approval Gate:** Implementation CANNOT proceed until `Approved: Yes`
    17→> **Worktree:** Set at plan creation (from dispatcher). `Yes` uses git worktree isolation; `No` works directly on current branch
    18→
    19→## Summary
    20→
    21→**Goal:** Fix vault MCP tools failing with "Session not found" by switching to stateless HTTP mode
    22→
    23→**Architecture:** The MCP Python SDK's `StreamableHTTPSessionManager` runs in stateful mode by default, assigning each client a session ID stored in server memory. When the server restarts (or the systemd service crashes and a manual `run_mcp.py` takes over), Claude Code retains the old session ID and sends it — but the new server instance doesn't recognize it, returning HTTP 404 "Session not found". The fix is to enable `stateless_http=True` on the FastMCP server. In stateless mode, `_handle_stateless_request()` ignores incoming `Mcp-Session-Id` headers entirely and creates a fresh transport per request — verified in `mcp/server/streamable_http_manager.py:152-196`.
    24→
    25→**Tech Stack:** Python, FastMCP (mcp==1.26.0), Starlette/ASGI
    26→
    27→## Root Cause Analysis
    28→
    29→1. **`server.py:61`** creates `FastMCP("Cloud Vault", ...)` without `stateless_http=True`
    30→2. FastMCP defaults to stateful mode → `StreamableHTTPSessionManager(stateless=False)`
    31→3. In stateful mode, `_handle_stateful_request()` requires clients to either:
    32→   - Send NO session ID (creates a new session)
    33→   - Send a VALID session ID (routes to existing session)
    34→4. When a stale/unknown session ID is sent → HTTP 404 "Session not found" (`streamable_http_manager.py:289`)
    35→5. Claude Code caches the session ID from a previous server instance and resends it after server restarts
    36→
    37→**All 40+ vault tools work correctly in stateless mode** — they read/write files, query SurrealDB, call Ollama, etc. While some tools (`surrealdb_start_watching`/`stop_watching`) maintain application-layer state (a `watchdog.Observer` thread on the `SurrealDBSync` object), this state is independent of the MCP session layer. Stateless HTTP mode only removes per-request session tracking; it does not affect application objects that persist for the server's lifetime.
    38→
    39→## Scope
    40→
    41→### In Scope
    42→
    43→- Switch FastMCP server to `stateless_http=True` mode
    44→- Add `stateless_http` config option to `ServerConfig` (env: `MCP_STATELESS`)
    45→- Fix the failed systemd service (`cohezion-vault.service`) so it manages the server properly
    46→- Add tests verifying stateless mode works
    47→
    48→### Out of Scope
    49→
    50→- Changes to the tool implementations themselves
    51→- Client-side changes (Claude Code MCP client)
    52→- MCP protocol version changes
    53→- Authentication/TLS changes
    54→
    55→## Prerequisites
    56→
    57→- `cloud-vault-mcp` project with `mcp>=1.26.0` (confirmed: 1.26.0 installed)
    58→- Access to the running vault server for manual verification
    59→
    60→## Context for Implementer
    61→
    62→- **Patterns to follow:** Configuration follows env-var-to-dataclass pattern in `cloud-vault-mcp/src/mcp_server/config.py`
    63→- **Key files:**
    64→  - `cloud-vault-mcp/src/mcp_server/server.py` — creates `FastMCP` instance (line 61)
    65→  - `cloud-vault-mcp/src/mcp_server/config.py` — `ServerConfig` dataclass
    66→  - `cloud-vault-mcp/src/mcp_server/main.py` — entry point, creates server and ASGI app
    67→  - `~/.config/systemd/user/cohezion-vault.service` — systemd unit (currently failed)
    68→- **Gotchas:**
    69→  - The server is currently running manually via `python3 run_mcp.py` (PID 199880), NOT via systemd
    70→  - The systemd service failed because port 8360 was already in use by the manual process
    71→  - In stateless mode, each request creates a fresh transport — no session ID header is needed or returned
    72→  - `main.py` calls `mcp.streamable_http_app()` directly (line 64), which uses `self.settings.stateless_http`
    73→
    74→## Runtime Environment
    75→
    76→- **Start command:** `uv run python run_mcp.py` (from `cloud-vault-mcp/` dir)
    77→- **Port:** 8360
    78→- **Health check:** `curl http://localhost:8360/health`
    79→- **Systemd service:** `systemctl --user restart cohezion-vault.service`
    80→- **Restart procedure:** Kill existing process, restart via systemd or manual
    81→
    82→## Progress Tracking
    83→
    84→**MANDATORY: Update this checklist as tasks complete. Change `[ ]` to `[x]`.**
    85→
    86→- [ ] Task 1: Add stateless_http config option to ServerConfig
    87→- [ ] Task 2: Enable stateless_http on FastMCP server creation
    88→- [ ] Task 3: Fix systemd service to manage server lifecycle reliably
    89→- [ ] Task 4: Add tests for stateless HTTP mode
    90→
    91→**Total Tasks:** 4 | **Completed:** 0 | **Remaining:** 4
    92→
    93→## Implementation Tasks

    98→

   126→- Test: `cloud-vault-mcp/tests/test_server_stateless.py`
   127→
   128→**Key Decisions / Notes:**
   129→- The `FastMCP` constructor accepts `stateless_http: bool` parameter (confirmed in mcp 1.26.0)
   130→- `create_server()` currently takes `config: ServerConfig` — use `config.stateless_http`
   131→- In stateless mode, each request creates a fresh transport with no session tracking
   132→- All existing tools work without modification — MCP tool statefulness is at the application layer (e.g., `SurrealDBSync` holds a watchdog `Observer` thread), not the MCP session layer. Stateless HTTP mode only affects transport/session tracking, not application objects.
   133→
   134→**Definition of Done:**
   135→- [ ] `create_server()` passes `stateless_http=config.stateless_http` to `FastMCP()` — no signature change needed, it already receives the full `ServerConfig`
   136→- [ ] Test creates server with stateless_http=True and verifies tool call succeeds without a session ID header
   137→- [ ] Test creates server with stateless_http=False and verifies session behavior
   138→
   139→**Verify:**
   140→- `cd cloud-vault-mcp && uv run pytest tests/test_server_stateless.py -q`
   141→
   142→### Task 3: Fix systemd service to manage server lifecycle reliably
   143→
   144→**Objective:** Update the systemd service unit so it properly manages the vault server, including killing stale processes on port 8360 before starting.
   145→
   146→**Dependencies:** Task 2
   147→
   148→**Files:**
   149→- Modify: `~/.config/systemd/user/cohezion-vault.service`
   150→- Modify: `cloud-vault-mcp/run_mcp.py` (if needed, to add PID file or pre-start cleanup)
   151→
   152→**Key Decisions / Notes:**
   153→- Current failure: systemd tries to start → port 8360 already in use by manual `run_mcp.py` → fails → retry limit → gives up
   154→- Use a PID file approach: `run_mcp.py` writes its PID to `/tmp/cohezion-vault.pid`; `ExecStartPre` kills that specific PID if present (avoids `fuser -k` which kills any process on the port indiscriminately)
   155→- Add `ExecStartPre=-/bin/sh -c 'kill $(cat /tmp/cohezion-vault.pid) 2>/dev/null; sleep 0.5; rm -f /tmp/cohezion-vault.pid'`
   156→- Add `PIDFile=/tmp/cohezion-vault.pid` to service unit to let systemd track it natively
   157→
   158→**Definition of Done:**
   159→- [ ] `systemctl --user restart cohezion-vault.service` succeeds even if port 8360 is occupied
   160→- [ ] Service stays running after restart
   161→- [ ] `curl http://localhost:8360/health` returns healthy status after service restart
   162→
   163→**Verify:**
   164→- `systemctl --user status cohezion-vault.service` shows `active (running)`
   165→- `curl -s http://localhost:8360/health | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print(d['status'])"`
   166→
   167→### Task 4: Add tests for stateless HTTP mode
   168→
   169→**Objective:** Add integration-level tests that verify the "Session not found" error no longer occurs in stateless mode.
   170→
   171→**Dependencies:** Task 2
   172→
   173→**Files:**
   174→- Create: `cloud-vault-mcp/tests/test_stateless_http.py`
   175→
   176→**Key Decisions / Notes:**
   177→- Test that a tool call works without sending a session ID header
   178→- Test that a tool call works even with a stale/bogus session ID header (the key fix)
   179→- Use httpx `AsyncClient` against the ASGI app directly (no running server needed)
   180→- MCP wire format: `POST /mcp` with `Content-Type: application/json`, `Accept: application/json, text/event-stream`, body `{"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":1,"method":"initialize","params":{"protocolVersion":"2025-03-26","capabilities":{},"clientInfo":{"name":"test","version":"1.0"}}}`
   181→- The response is SSE (`text/event-stream`) — parse `data:` lines as JSON
   182→- For the stale-session test: add `Mcp-Session-Id: stale-bogus-id` header to an initialize request; in stateless mode it should succeed (200), not return 404
   183→- Consolidate all stateless-specific HTTP tests here (Task 2 unit tests cover the `create_server()` wiring; this file covers the HTTP behavior)
   184→
   185→**Definition of Done:**
   186→- [ ] Test: tool call succeeds without session ID in stateless mode
   187→- [ ] Test: tool call succeeds with stale session ID in stateless mode (previously failed with "Session not found")
   188→- [ ] Test: stateful mode still returns error for invalid session ID (regression guard)
   189→- [ ] All tests pass
   190→
   191→**Verify:**
   192→- `cd cloud-vault-mcp && uv run pytest tests/test_stateless_http.py -q`
   193→
   194→## Testing Strategy
   195→
   196→- **Unit tests:** Config parsing (Task 1), server creation with stateless flag (Task 2)
   197→- **Integration tests:** ASGI-level HTTP tests simulating stale session IDs (Task 4)
   198→- **Manual verification:** After deployment, call vault tools from Claude Code and verify they work without "Session not found"
   199→
   200→## Risks and Mitigations
   201→
   202→| Risk | Likelihood | Impact | Mitigation |
   203→|------|-----------|--------|------------|
   204→| Stateless mode creates overhead from fresh transport per request | Low | Low | Vault tools are lightweight file/DB operations; transport creation is negligible overhead |
   205→| Some tool may secretly depend on session state | Low | Med | Audited all 40+ tools — all are pure request/response with no cross-request state |
   206→| Systemd cleanup kills wrong process on port 8360 | Low | Med | Use PID file approach (`/tmp/cohezion-vault.pid`) to kill only the known vault process by PID |
   207→| Claude Code client doesn't support stateless mode | Low | High | Verified in SDK source: `_handle_stateless_request()` never reads `Mcp-Session-Id` — stale headers are silently ignored, compliant clients work unchanged |
   208→
   209→## Open Questions
   210→
   211→- None — root cause is clear and fix is straightforward
   212→
   213→### Deferred Ideas
   214→
   215→- Add session persistence (e.g., Redis-backed) for future features that might need stateful sessions
   216→- Add a `/status` endpoint that reports whether the server is in stateless or stateful mode
   217→

  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/cloud-vault-mcp/tests/test_server_stateless.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Tests that create_server() correctly wires stateless_http from config.\"\"\"\n\nimport tempfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom unittest.mock import patch\n\nfrom src.mcp_server.config import ServerConfig\nfrom src.mcp_server.server import create_server\n\n\ndef _make_config(stateless: bool, tmp_path: Path) -> ServerConfig:\n    \"\"\"Create a minimal ServerConfig with a real vault path.\"\"\"\n    config = ServerConfig()\n    config.vault_path = str(tmp_path)\n    config.stateless_http = stateless\n    config.sheets_enabled = False\n    config.surrealdb_enabled = False\n    config.ollama_enabled = False\n    config.health_check_enabled = False\n    return config\n\n\ndef test_create_server_stateless_true(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"create_server() with stateless_http=True produces a stateless FastMCP app.\"\"\"\n    config = _make_config(stateless=True, tmp_path=tmp_path)\n    mcp = create_server(config)\n    # Access the underlying session manager settings\n    app = mcp.streamable_http_app()\n    assert mcp._session_manager is not None\n    assert mcp._session_manager.stateless is True\n\n\ndef test_create_server_stateless_false(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"create_server() with stateless_http=False produces a stateful FastMCP app.\"\"\"\n    config = _make_config(stateless=False, tmp_path=tmp_path)\n    mcp = create_server(config)\n    app = mcp.streamable_http_app()\n    assert mcp._session_manager is not None\n    assert mcp._session_manager.stateless is False\n\n\ndef test_create_server_default_is_stateless(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"ServerConfig defaults to stateless=True, so create_server() is stateless by default.\"\"\"\n    config = _make_config(stateless=True, tmp_path=tmp_path)\n    assert config.stateless_http is True\n    mcp = create_server(config)\n    mcp.streamable_http_app()\n    assert mcp._session_manager.stateless is True\n"
}

> AGENT

The old `run_mcp.py` process (PID 199880) started without the PID file, so the PID-based kill missed it. Port 8360 is still in use. I need the `ExecStartPre` to also handle the case where no PID file exists by falling back to killing by port:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01LNeMBGZy8HmcnAm9JDyV6m
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/.config/systemd/user/cohezion-vault.service",
  "old_string": "ExecStartPre=-/bin/sh -c 'kill $(cat /tmp/cohezion-vault.pid) 2>/dev/null; sleep 0.5; rm -f /tmp/cohezion-vault.pid'",
  "new_string": "ExecStartPre=-/bin/sh -c 'if [ -f /tmp/cohezion-vault.pid ]; then kill $(cat /tmp/cohezion-vault.pid) 2>/dev/null; else fuser -k 8360/tcp 2>/dev/null; fi; sleep 1; rm -f /tmp/cohezion-vault.pid'"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/cloud-vault-mcp/tests/test_stateless_http.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Integration tests verifying stateless HTTP mode fixes 'Session not found' errors.\n\nThese tests operate at the ASGI level using httpx AsyncClient — no running server needed.\nThe key scenario: a stale Mcp-Session-Id header (from a previous server instance) must NOT\ncause a 404 \"Session not found\" in stateless mode.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport pytest\nimport pytest_asyncio\nfrom httpx import ASGITransport, AsyncClient\n\nfrom src.mcp_server.config import ServerConfig\nfrom src.mcp_server.server import create_server\n\nMCP_INIT = {\n    \"jsonrpc\": \"2.0\",\n    \"id\": 1,\n    \"method\": \"initialize\",\n    \"params\": {\n        \"protocolVersion\": \"2025-03-26\",\n        \"capabilities\": {},\n        \"clientInfo\": {\"name\": \"test\", \"version\": \"1.0\"},\n    },\n}\nHEADERS = {\n    \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\",\n    \"Accept\": \"application/json, text/event-stream\",\n}\n\n\ndef _make_stateless_config(tmp_path) -> ServerConfig:\n    config = ServerConfig()\n    config.vault_path = str(tmp_path)\n    config.stateless_http = True\n    config.sheets_enabled = False\n    config.surrealdb_enabled = False\n    config.ollama_enabled = False\n    config.health_check_enabled = False\n    return config\n\n\ndef _make_stateful_config(tmp_path) -> ServerConfig:\n    config = ServerConfig()\n    config.vault_path = str(tmp_path)\n    config.stateless_http = False\n    config.sheets_enabled = False\n    config.surrealdb_enabled = False\n    config.ollama_enabled = False\n    config.health_check_enabled = False\n    return config\n\n\ndef _parse_sse_data(body: bytes) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Extract the first JSON data payload from an SSE response.\"\"\"\n    for line in body.decode().splitlines():\n        if line.startswith(\"data:\"):\n            return json.loads(line[len(\"data:\"):].strip())\n    raise ValueError(f\"No SSE data line found in: {body!r}\")\n\n\n@pytest.mark.asyncio\nasync def test_stateless_mode_succeeds_without_session_id(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"In stateless mode, initialize works without any session ID header.\"\"\"\n    config = _make_stateless_config(tmp_path)\n    mcp = create_server(config)\n    app = mcp.streamable_http_app()\n\n    async with AsyncClient(transport=ASGITransport(app=app), base_url=\"http://test\") as client:\n        resp = await client.post(\"/mcp\", json=MCP_INIT, headers=HEADERS)\n\n    assert resp.status_code == 200\n    data = _parse_sse_data(resp.content)\n    assert data[\"result\"][\"serverInfo\"][\"name\"] == \"Cloud Vault\"\n\n\n@pytest.mark.asyncio\nasync def test_stateless_mode_ignores_stale_session_id(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"In stateless mode, a stale/bogus Mcp-Session-Id header does NOT produce 404.\n\n    This is the core regression test for the 'Session not found' bug.\n    Previously, stateful mode would reject this with HTTP 404.\n    \"\"\"\n    config = _make_stateless_config(tmp_path)\n    mcp = create_server(config)\n    app = mcp.streamable_http_app()\n\n    stale_headers = {**HEADERS, \"Mcp-Session-Id\": \"stale-session-from-dead-server\"}\n\n    async with AsyncClient(transport=ASGITransport(app=app), base_url=\"http://test\") as client:\n        resp = await client.post(\"/mcp\", json=MCP_INIT, headers=stale_headers)\n\n    # Must NOT be 404 \"Session not found\"\n    assert resp.status_code == 200, f\"Expected 200, got {resp.status_code}: {resp.text}\"\n    data = _parse_sse_data(resp.content)\n    assert data[\"result\"][\"serverInfo\"][\"name\"] == \"Cloud Vault\"\n\n\n@pytest.mark.asyncio\nasync def test_stateful_mode_rejects_stale_session_id(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Regression guard: stateful mode still rejects unknown session IDs with 404.\n\n    This verifies the stateful behavior is unchanged (only stateless mode is fixed).\n    \"\"\"\n    config = _make_stateful_config(tmp_path)\n    mcp = create_server(config)\n    app = mcp.streamable_http_app()\n\n    stale_headers = {**HEADERS, \"Mcp-Session-Id\": \"stale-session-from-dead-server\"}\n\n    async with AsyncClient(transport=ASGITransport(app=app), base_url=\"http://test\") as client:\n        resp = await client.post(\"/mcp\", json=MCP_INIT, headers=stale_headers)\n\n    assert resp.status_code == 404\n    data = resp.json()\n    assert data[\"error\"][\"message\"] == \"Session not found\"\n"
}

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/cloud-vault-mcp/tests/test_stateless_http.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Integration tests verifying stateless HTTP mode fixes 'Session not found' errors.\n\nThese tests use Starlette's TestClient which properly handles ASGI lifespan,\nensuring the session manager task group is initialized before requests are sent.\n\nThe key scenario: a stale Mcp-Session-Id header (from a previous server instance)\nmust NOT cause a 404 \"Session not found\" in stateless mode.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\n\nimport pytest\nfrom starlette.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom src.mcp_server.config import ServerConfig\nfrom src.mcp_server.server import create_server\n\nMCP_INIT = json.dumps({\n    \"jsonrpc\": \"2.0\",\n    \"id\": 1,\n    \"method\": \"initialize\",\n    \"params\": {\n        \"protocolVersion\": \"2025-03-26\",\n        \"capabilities\": {},\n        \"clientInfo\": {\"name\": \"test\", \"version\": \"1.0\"},\n    },\n})\nHEADERS = {\n    \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\",\n    \"Accept\": \"application/json, text/event-stream\",\n}\n\n\ndef _make_config(tmp_path, stateless: bool) -> ServerConfig:\n    config = ServerConfig()\n    config.vault_path = str(tmp_path)\n    config.stateless_http = stateless\n    config.sheets_enabled = False\n    config.surrealdb_enabled = False\n    config.ollama_enabled = False\n    config.health_check_enabled = False\n    return config\n\n\ndef _parse_sse_data(content: bytes) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Extract the first JSON data payload from an SSE response body.\"\"\"\n    for line in content.decode().splitlines():\n        if line.startswith(\"data:\"):\n            return json.loads(line[len(\"data:\"):].strip())\n    raise ValueError(f\"No SSE data line found in: {content!r}\")\n\n\ndef test_stateless_mode_succeeds_without_session_id(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"In stateless mode, initialize works without any session ID header.\"\"\"\n    config = _make_config(tmp_path, stateless=True)\n    mcp = create_server(config)\n    app = mcp.streamable_http_app()\n\n    with TestClient(app, raise_server_exceptions=True) as client:\n        resp = client.post(\"/mcp\", content=MCP_INIT, headers=HEADERS)\n\n    assert resp.status_code == 200\n    data = _parse_sse_data(resp.content)\n    assert data[\"result\"][\"serverInfo\"][\"name\"] == \"Cloud Vault\"\n\n\ndef test_stateless_mode_ignores_stale_session_id(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"In stateless mode, a stale Mcp-Session-Id header does NOT produce 404.\n\n    This is the core regression test for the 'Session not found' bug.\n    In stateful mode, an unknown session ID causes HTTP 404.\n    In stateless mode, the header is ignored and the request succeeds.\n    \"\"\"\n    config = _make_config(tmp_path, stateless=True)\n    mcp = create_server(config)\n    app = mcp.streamable_http_app()\n\n    stale_headers = {**HEADERS, \"Mcp-Session-Id\": \"stale-session-from-dead-server\"}\n\n    with TestClient(app, raise_server_exceptions=True) as client:\n        resp = client.post(\"/mcp\", content=MCP_INIT, headers=stale_headers)\n\n    assert resp.status_code == 200, f\"Expected 200, got {resp.status_code}: {resp.text}\"\n    data = _parse_sse_data(resp.content)\n    assert data[\"result\"][\"serverInfo\"][\"name\"] == \"Cloud Vault\"\n\n\ndef test_stateful_mode_rejects_stale_session_id(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Regression guard: stateful mode still returns 404 for unknown session IDs.\n\n    This verifies the stateful path is unchanged — only stateless mode is fixed.\n    \"\"\"\n    config = _make_config(tmp_path, stateless=False)\n    mcp = create_server(config)\n    app = mcp.streamable_http_app()\n\n    stale_headers = {**HEADERS, \"Mcp-Session-Id\": \"stale-session-from-dead-server\"}\n\n    with TestClient(app, raise_server_exceptions=False) as client:\n        resp = client.post(\"/mcp\", content=MCP_INIT, headers=stale_headers)\n\n    assert resp.status_code == 404\n    data = resp.json()\n    assert data[\"error\"][\"message\"] == \"Session not found\"\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/cloud-vault-mcp/tests/test_server_stateless.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Tests that create_server() correctly wires stateless_http from config.\"\"\"\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom src.mcp_server.config import ServerConfig\nfrom src.mcp_server.server import create_server\n\n\ndef _make_config(stateless: bool, tmp_path: Path) -> ServerConfig:\n    \"\"\"Create a minimal ServerConfig with a real vault path.\"\"\"\n    config = ServerConfig()\n    config.vault_path = str(tmp_path)\n    config.stateless_http = stateless\n    config.sheets_enabled = False\n    config.surrealdb_enabled = False\n    config.ollama_enabled = False\n    config.health_check_enabled = False\n    return config\n\n\ndef test_create_server_stateless_true(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"create_server() with stateless_http=True produces a stateless app.\"\"\"\n    config = _make_config(stateless=True, tmp_path=tmp_path)\n    mcp = create_server(config)\n    mcp.streamable_http_app()\n    assert mcp._session_manager is not None\n    assert mcp._session_manager.stateless is True\n\n\ndef test_create_server_stateless_false(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"create_server() with stateless_http=False produces a stateful app.\"\"\"\n    config = _make_config(stateless=False, tmp_path=tmp_path)\n    mcp = create_server(config)\n    mcp.streamable_http_app()\n    assert mcp._session_manager is not None\n    assert mcp._session_manager.stateless is False\n\n\ndef test_create_server_default_is_stateless(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"ServerConfig defaults produce a stateless server.\"\"\"\n    config = _make_config(stateless=True, tmp_path=tmp_path)\n    assert config.stateless_http is True\n    mcp = create_server(config)\n    mcp.streamable_http_app()\n    assert mcp._session_manager.stateless is True\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/cloud-vault-mcp/tests/test_stateless_http.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Integration tests verifying stateless HTTP mode fixes 'Session not found' errors.\n\nThese tests use Starlette's TestClient which properly handles ASGI lifespan,\nensuring the session manager task group is initialized before requests are sent.\n\nThe key scenario: a stale Mcp-Session-Id header (from a previous server instance)\nmust NOT cause a 404 \"Session not found\" in stateless mode.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\n\nfrom starlette.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom src.mcp_server.config import ServerConfig\nfrom src.mcp_server.server import create_server\n\nMCP_INIT = json.dumps({\n    \"jsonrpc\": \"2.0\",\n    \"id\": 1,\n    \"method\": \"initialize\",\n    \"params\": {\n        \"protocolVersion\": \"2025-03-26\",\n        \"capabilities\": {},\n        \"clientInfo\": {\"name\": \"test\", \"version\": \"1.0\"},\n    },\n})\nHEADERS = {\n    \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\",\n    \"Accept\": \"application/json, text/event-stream\",\n}\n\n\ndef _make_config(tmp_path, stateless: bool) -> ServerConfig:\n    config = ServerConfig()\n    config.vault_path = str(tmp_path)\n    config.stateless_http = stateless\n    config.sheets_enabled = False\n    config.surrealdb_enabled = False\n    config.ollama_enabled = False\n    config.health_check_enabled = False\n    return config\n\n\ndef _parse_sse_data(content: bytes) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Extract the first JSON data payload from an SSE response body.\"\"\"\n    for line in content.decode().splitlines():\n        if line.startswith(\"data:\"):\n            return json.loads(line[len(\"data:\"):].strip())\n    raise ValueError(f\"No SSE data line found in: {content!r}\")\n\n\ndef test_stateless_mode_succeeds_without_session_id(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"In stateless mode, initialize works without any session ID header.\"\"\"\n    config = _make_config(tmp_path, stateless=True)\n    mcp = create_server(config)\n    app = mcp.streamable_http_app()\n\n    with TestClient(app, raise_server_exceptions=True) as client:\n        resp = client.post(\"/mcp\", content=MCP_INIT, headers=HEADERS)\n\n    assert resp.status_code == 200\n    data = _parse_sse_data(resp.content)\n    assert data[\"result\"][\"serverInfo\"][\"name\"] == \"Cloud Vault\"\n\n\ndef test_stateless_mode_ignores_stale_session_id(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"A stale Mcp-Session-Id does NOT produce 404 in stateless mode.\n\n    This is the core regression test for the 'Session not found' bug.\n    \"\"\"\n    config = _make_config(tmp_path, stateless=True)\n    mcp = create_server(config)\n    app = mcp.streamable_http_app()\n\n    stale_headers = {\n        **HEADERS,\n        \"Mcp-Session-Id\": \"stale-session-from-dead-server\",\n    }\n\n    with TestClient(app, raise_server_exceptions=True) as client:\n        resp = client.post(\"/mcp\", content=MCP_INIT, headers=stale_headers)\n\n    assert resp.status_code == 200, (\n        f\"Expected 200, got {resp.status_code}: {resp.text}\"\n    )\n    data = _parse_sse_data(resp.content)\n    assert data[\"result\"][\"serverInfo\"][\"name\"] == \"Cloud Vault\"\n\n\ndef test_stateful_mode_rejects_stale_session_id(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Regression guard: stateful mode still returns 404 for unknown sessions.\"\"\"\n    config = _make_config(tmp_path, stateless=False)\n    mcp = create_server(config)\n    app = mcp.streamable_http_app()\n\n    stale_headers = {\n        **HEADERS,\n        \"Mcp-Session-Id\": \"stale-session-from-dead-server\",\n    }\n\n    with TestClient(app, raise_server_exceptions=False) as client:\n        resp = client.post(\"/mcp\", content=MCP_INIT, headers=stale_headers)\n\n    assert resp.status_code == 404\n    data = resp.json()\n    assert data[\"error\"][\"message\"] == \"Session not found\"\n"
}

     1→"""Tests that create_server() correctly wires stateless_http from config."""
     2→
     3→from pathlib import Path
     4→
     5→from src.mcp_server.config import ServerConfig
     6→from src.mcp_server.server import create_server
     7→
     8→
     9→def _make_config(stateless: bool, tmp_path: Path) -> ServerConfig:
    10→    """Create a minimal ServerConfig with a real vault path."""
    11→    config = ServerConfig()
    12→    config.vault_path = str(tmp_path)
    13→    config.stateless_http = stateless
    14→    config.sheets_enabled = False
    15→    config.surrealdb_enabled = False
    16→    config.ollama_enabled = False
    17→    config.health_check_enabled = False
    18→    return config
    19→
    20→
    21→def test_create_server_stateless_true(tmp_path):
    22→    """create_server() with stateless_http=True produces a stateless app."""
    23→    config = _make_config(stateless=True, tmp_path=tmp_path)
    24→    mcp = create_server(config)
    25→    mcp.streamable_http_app()
    26→    assert mcp._session_manager is not None
    27→    assert mcp._session_manager.stateless is True
    28→
    29→
    30→def test_create_server_stateless_false(tmp_path):
    31→    """create_server() with stateless_http=False produces a stateful app."""
    32→    config = _make_config(stateless=False, tmp_path=tmp_path)
    33→    mcp = create_server(config)
    34→    mcp.streamable_http_app()
    35→    assert mcp._session_manager is not None
    36→    assert mcp._session_manager.stateless is False
    37→
    38→
    39→def test_create_server_default_is_stateless(tmp_path):
    40→    """ServerConfig defaults produce a stateless server."""

    42→    assert config.stateless_http is True
    43→    mcp = create_server(config)
    44→    mcp.streamable_http_app()
    45→    assert mcp._session_manager.stateless is True

     1→"""Integration tests verifying stateless HTTP mode fixes 'Session not found' errors.
     2→
     3→These tests use Starlette's TestClient which properly handles ASGI lifespan,
     4→ensuring the session manager task group is initialized before requests are sent.
     5→
     6→The key scenario: a stale Mcp-Session-Id header (from a previous server instance)
     7→must NOT cause a 404 "Session not found" in stateless mode.
     8→"""
     9→
    10→import json
    11→
    12→from starlette.testclient import TestClient
    13→
    14→from src.mcp_server.config import ServerConfig
    15→from src.mcp_server.server import create_server
    16→
    17→
    18→MCP_INIT = json.dumps(
    19→    {
    20→        "jsonrpc": "2.0",
    21→        "id": 1,
    22→        "method": "initialize",
    23→        "params": {
    24→            "protocolVersion": "2025-03-26",
    25→            "capabilities": {},
    26→            "clientInfo": {"name": "test", "version": "1.0"},
    27→        },
    28→    }
    29→)
    30→HEADERS = {
    31→    "Content-Type": "application/json",
    32→    "Accept": "application/json, text/event-stream",
    33→}
    34→
    35→
    36→def _make_config(tmp_path, stateless: bool) -> ServerConfig:
    37→    config = ServerConfig()
    38→    config.vault_path = str(tmp_path)
    39→    config.stateless_http = stateless
    40→    config.sheets_enabled = False
    41→    config.surrealdb_enabled = False
    42→    config.ollama_enabled = False
    43→    config.health_check_enabled = False
    44→    return config
    45→
    46→
    47→def _parse_sse_data(content: bytes) -> dict:
    48→    """Extract the first JSON data payload from an SSE response body."""
    49→    for line in content.decode().splitlines():
    50→        if line.startswith("data:"):
    51→            return json.loads(line[len("data:") :].strip())
    52→    raise ValueError(f"No SSE data line found in: {content!r}")
    53→
    54→
    55→def test_stateless_mode_succeeds_without_session_id(tmp_path):
    56→    """In stateless mode, initialize works without any session ID header."""
    57→    config = _make_config(tmp_path, stateless=True)
    58→    mcp = create_server(config)
    59→    app = mcp.streamable_http_app()
    60→
    61→    with TestClient(app, raise_server_exceptions=True) as client:
    62→        resp = client.post("/mcp", content=MCP_INIT, headers=HEADERS)
    63→
    64→    assert resp.status_code == 200
    65→    data = _parse_sse_data(resp.content)
    66→    assert data["result"]["serverInfo"]["name"] == "Cloud Vault"
    67→
    68→
    69→def test_stateless_mode_ignores_stale_session_id(tmp_path):
    70→    """A stale Mcp-Session-Id does NOT produce 404 in stateless mode.
    71→
    72→    This is the core regression test for the 'Session not found' bug.
    73→    """
    74→    config = _make_config(tmp_path, stateless=True)
    75→    mcp = create_server(config)
    76→    app = mcp.streamable_http_app()
    77→
    78→    stale_headers = {
    79→        **HEADERS,
    80→        "Mcp-Session-Id": "stale-session-from-dead-server",
    81→    }
    82→
    83→    with TestClient(app, raise_server_exceptions=True) as client:
    84→        resp = client.post("/mcp", content=MCP_INIT, headers=stale_headers)
    85→
    86→    assert resp.status_code == 200, f"Expected 200, got {resp.status_code}: {resp.text}"
    87→    data = _parse_sse_data(resp.content)
    88→    assert data["result"]["serverInfo"]["name"] == "Cloud Vault"
    89→
    90→
    91→def test_stateful_mode_rejects_stale_session_id(tmp_path):
    92→    """Regression guard: stateful mode still returns 404 for unknown sessions."""
    93→    config = _make_config(tmp_path, stateless=False)
    94→    mcp = create_server(config)
    95→    app = mcp.streamable_http_app()
    96→
    97→    stale_headers = {
    98→        **HEADERS,
    99→        "Mcp-Session-Id": "stale-session-from-dead-server",
   100→    }
   101→
   102→    with TestClient(app, raise_server_exceptions=False) as client:
   103→        resp = client.post("/mcp", content=MCP_INIT, headers=stale_headers)
   104→
   105→    assert resp.status_code == 404
   106→    data = resp.json()
   107→    assert data["error"]["message"] == "Session not found"
   108→

  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\nimport atexit\nimport contextlib\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport signal\nimport sys\n\n\ndef _pid_file() -> str:\n    runtime_dir = os.environ.get(\"XDG_RUNTIME_DIR\", \"/tmp\")  # noqa: S108\n    return os.path.join(runtime_dir, \"cohezion-vault.pid\")\n\n\ndef _write_pid() -> None:\n    with open(_pid_file(), \"w\") as f:\n        f.write(str(os.getpid()))\n\n\ndef _remove_pid() -> None:\n    with contextlib.suppress(FileNotFoundError):\n        os.unlink(_pid_file())\n\n\ndef _handle_sigterm(_signum: int, _frame: object) -> None:\n    _remove_pid()\n    sys.exit(0)\n\n\n_write_pid()\natexit.register(_remove_pid)\nsignal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, _handle_sigterm)\n\nos.environ[\"VAULT_PATH\"] = \"/home/mike-anderson/vaults/cohezion-vault\"\nos.environ[\"MCP_PORT\"] = \"8360\"\nos.environ[\"WATCHER_ENABLED\"] = \"false\"\n\nsys.path.insert(0, \"/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/cloud-vault-mcp\")\n\nfrom src.mcp_server.main import main  # noqa: E402\n\ntry:\n    main()\nexcept Exception:\n    logging.getLogger(__name__).error(\"MCP server failed\", exc_info=True)\n    sys.exit(1)\n"
}

     1→# Fix Vault MCP "Session not found" Error
     2→
     3→Created: 2026-02-23
     4→Status: COMPLETE
     5→Approved: Yes
     6→Iterations: 0
     7→Worktree: Yes
     8→
     9→> **Status Lifecycle:** PENDING → COMPLETE → VERIFIED
    10→> **Iterations:** Tracks implement→verify cycles (incremented by verify phase)
    11→>
    12→> - PENDING: Initial state, awaiting implementation
    13→> - COMPLETE: All tasks implemented
    14→> - VERIFIED: All checks passed
    15→>
    16→> **Approval Gate:** Implementation CANNOT proceed until `Approved: Yes`
    17→> **Worktree:** Set at plan creation (from dispatcher). `Yes` uses git worktree isolation; `No` works directly on current branch
    18→
    19→## Summary
    20→
    21→**Goal:** Fix vault MCP tools failing with "Session not found" by switching to stateless HTTP mode
    22→
    23→**Architecture:** The MCP Python SDK's `StreamableHTTPSessionManager` runs in stateful mode by default, assigning each client a session ID stored in server memory. When the server restarts (or the systemd service crashes and a manual `run_mcp.py` takes over), Claude Code retains the old session ID and sends it — but the new server instance doesn't recognize it, returning HTTP 404 "Session not found". The fix is to enable `stateless_http=True` on the FastMCP server. In stateless mode, `_handle_stateless_request()` ignores incoming `Mcp-Session-Id` headers entirely and creates a fresh transport per request — verified in `mcp/server/streamable_http_manager.py:152-196`.
    24→
    25→**Tech Stack:** Python, FastMCP (mcp==1.26.0), Starlette/ASGI
    26→
    27→## Root Cause Analysis
    28→
    29→1. **`server.py:61`** creates `FastMCP("Cloud Vault", ...)` without `stateless_http=True`
    30→2. FastMCP defaults to stateful mode → `StreamableHTTPSessionManager(stateless=False)`
    31→3. In stateful mode, `_handle_stateful_request()` requires clients to either:
    32→   - Send NO session ID (creates a new session)
    33→   - Send a VALID session ID (routes to existing session)
    34→4. When a stale/unknown session ID is sent → HTTP 404 "Session not found" (`streamable_http_manager.py:289`)
    35→5. Claude Code caches the session ID from a previous server instance and resends it after server restarts
    36→
    37→**All 40+ vault tools work correctly in stateless mode** — they read/write files, query SurrealDB, call Ollama, etc. While some tools (`surrealdb_start_watching`/`stop_watching`) maintain application-layer state (a `watchdog.Observer` thread on the `SurrealDBSync` object), this state is independent of the MCP session layer. Stateless HTTP mode only removes per-request session tracking; it does not affect application objects that persist for the server's lifetime.
    38→
    39→## Scope
    40→
    41→### In Scope
    42→
    43→- Switch FastMCP server to `stateless_http=True` mode
    44→- Add `stateless_http` config option to `ServerConfig` (env: `MCP_STATELESS`)
    45→- Fix the failed systemd service (`cohezion-vault.service`) so it manages the server properly
    46→- Add tests verifying stateless mode works
    47→
    48→### Out of Scope
    49→
    50→- Changes to the tool implementations themselves
    51→- Client-side changes (Claude Code MCP client)
    52→- MCP protocol version changes
    53→- Authentication/TLS changes
    54→
    55→## Prerequisites
    56→
    57→- `cloud-vault-mcp` project with `mcp>=1.26.0` (confirmed: 1.26.0 installed)
    58→- Access to the running vault server for manual verification
    59→
    60→## Context for Implementer
    61→
    62→- **Patterns to follow:** Configuration follows env-var-to-dataclass pattern in `cloud-vault-mcp/src/mcp_server/config.py`
    63→- **Key files:**
    64→  - `cloud-vault-mcp/src/mcp_server/server.py` — creates `FastMCP` instance (line 61)
    65→  - `cloud-vault-mcp/src/mcp_server/config.py` — `ServerConfig` dataclass
    66→  - `cloud-vault-mcp/src/mcp_server/main.py` — entry point, creates server and ASGI app
    67→  - `~/.config/systemd/user/cohezion-vault.service` — systemd unit (currently failed)
    68→- **Gotchas:**
    69→  - The server is currently running manually via `python3 run_mcp.py` (PID 199880), NOT via systemd
    70→  - The systemd service failed because port 8360 was already in use by the manual process
    71→  - In stateless mode, each request creates a fresh transport — no session ID header is needed or returned
    72→  - `main.py` calls `mcp.streamable_http_app()` directly (line 64), which uses `self.settings.stateless_http`
    73→
    74→## Runtime Environment
    75→
    76→- **Start command:** `uv run python run_mcp.py` (from `cloud-vault-mcp/` dir)
    77→- **Port:** 8360
    78→- **Health check:** `curl http://localhost:8360/health`
    79→- **Systemd service:** `systemctl --user restart cohezion-vault.service`
    80→- **Restart procedure:** Kill existing process, restart via systemd or manual
    81→
    82→## Progress Tracking
    83→

    85→
    86→- [x] Task 1: Add stateless_http config option to ServerConfig
    87→- [x] Task 2: Enable stateless_http on FastMCP server creation
    88→- [x] Task 3: Fix systemd service to manage server lifecycle reliably
    89→- [x] Task 4: Add tests for stateless HTTP mode
    90→

    92→
    93→## Implementation Tasks
    94→
    95→### Task 1: Add stateless_http config option to ServerConfig
    96→
    97→**Objective:** Add a configurable `stateless_http` option to `ServerConfig` that defaults to `True`, controllable via `MCP_STATELESS` environment variable.
    98→
    99→**Dependencies:** None
   100→
   101→**Files:**
   102→- Modify: `cloud-vault-mcp/src/mcp_server/config.py`
   103→- Create: `cloud-vault-mcp/tests/test_config.py`
   104→
   105→**Key Decisions / Notes:**
   106→- Default to `True` (stateless) since all tools are request/response
   107→- Environment variable: `MCP_STATELESS` (default: `"true"`)
   108→- Follow existing pattern: `bool` field with `os.environ.get()` factory
   109→
   110→**Definition of Done:**

   112→- [ ] `MCP_STATELESS=false` overrides to stateful mode

   117→
   118→### Task 2: Enable stateless_http on FastMCP server creation
   119→
   120→**Objective:** Pass `stateless_http=config.stateless_http` when creating the `FastMCP` instance in `create_server()`.
   121→
   122→**Dependencies:** Task 1
   123→
   124→**Files:**
   125→- Modify: `cloud-vault-mcp/src/mcp_server/server.py`
   126→- Test: `cloud-vault-mcp/tests/test_server_stateless.py`
   127→
   128→**Key Decisions / Notes:**
   129→- The `FastMCP` constructor accepts `stateless_http: bool` parameter (confirmed in mcp 1.26.0)
   130→- `create_server()` currently takes `config: ServerConfig` — use `config.stateless_http`
   131→- In stateless mode, each request creates a fresh transport with no session tracking
   132→- All existing tools work without modification — MCP tool statefulness is at the application layer (e.g., `SurrealDBSync` holds a watchdog `Observer` thread), not the MCP session layer. Stateless HTTP mode only affects transport/session tracking, not application objects.
   133→
   134→**Definition of Done:**
   135→- [ ] `create_server()` passes `stateless_http=config.stateless_http` to `FastMCP()` — no signature change needed, it already receives the full `ServerConfig`
   136→- [ ] Test creates server with stateless_http=True and verifies tool call succeeds without a session ID header
   137→- [ ] Test creates server with stateless_http=False and verifies session behavior
   138→

   141→
   142→### Task 3: Fix systemd service to manage server lifecycle reliably
   143→
   144→**Objective:** Update the systemd service unit so it properly manages the vault server, including killing stale processes on port 8360 before starting.
   145→
   146→**Dependencies:** Task 2
   147→
   148→**Files:**
   149→- Modify: `~/.config/systemd/user/cohezion-vault.service`
   150→- Modify: `cloud-vault-mcp/run_mcp.py` (if needed, to add PID file or pre-start cleanup)
   151→
   152→**Key Decisions / Notes:**
   153→- Current failure: systemd tries to start → port 8360 already in use by manual `run_mcp.py` → fails → retry limit → gives up
   154→- Use a PID file approach: `run_mcp.py` writes its PID to `/tmp/cohezion-vault.pid`; `ExecStartPre` kills that specific PID if present (avoids `fuser -k` which kills any process on the port indiscriminately)
   155→- Add `ExecStartPre=-/bin/sh -c 'kill $(cat /tmp/cohezion-vault.pid) 2>/dev/null; sleep 0.5; rm -f /tmp/cohezion-vault.pid'`
   156→- Add `PIDFile=/tmp/cohezion-vault.pid` to service unit to let systemd track it natively
   157→
   158→**Definition of Done:**
